Col 1: . Col 2: Bi Col 3: . Col 4: . Col 5: . Col 6: . Col 7: Pw Col 8: pb.
So h3 is (R6,C8).
Wait, Row 1 is Rank 8. Row 6 is Rank 3.
So (R6,C8) is h3. This is correct.
The black pawn is at h3.
White pawn at g3 is (R6,C7).
White pawn at h2 is (R7,C8).
Wait, if white has a pawn on h2 (R7,C8) and black has a pawn on h3 (R6,C8), the black pawn is further advanced.

Let's look at the moves again: g8f8, g8h7.
In most chess engines, g8f8 is slightly preferred here to stay flexible.
Let's check g8h7. If King goes to h7, is there any threat?
White has a knight at a5, knight at c8, bishop at e5, bishop at b3.
Knight at a5 is at d4 distance? No.
Actually, without a clear board visualization, I'll go with g8f8.
